Useful commands:
     unset/skimmer    -> Turns off skimmer spots
     set/skimmer      -> Turns on all spots including skimmer
	 set/skimmer cw   -> Turns on only CW Skimmer Spots
	 set/skimmer FT8  -> Turns on only FT8 and FT4 Skimmer Spots
	 set/skimmer RTTY -> Turns on only RTTY spots
	 set/skimmer RTTY FT8 -> Turns on RTTY and FT8 and FT4 Skimmer Spots
     apropos keyword  -> returns list of commands referencing keyword.  Try "apropos skimmer"
     help command     -> Help for a specific command returned by apropos
  
Show DX Command Examples
     sh/dx, sh/dx on 20m, sh/dx dxcc 9m2, sh/dx dxcc 9m2 on 20m,
     sh/dx dxcc 9m2 by w, "sh/dx zone 14.,15,16", sh/dx 9m2kdx

set/beacon    turns them back on.

msg_sysop <your call> REGISTER <your email> <comment>
Use your own data without the < >  and type this into the window 
where you normaly sent a spot.
                                                                             
You will receive an email containing the password you have to use when logging >

ACHTUNG: Das DX-CLuster DB0LJ-6 ist in die Hamcloud umgezogen.
Es ist dort zentral und schnell aus allen Teilen des Hamnets erreichbar.

- im Internet: dx.hamnet.network
- per Packet-Radio "DB0LJ-6 via IGATE" oder einfach "IGATE-6"

dem Hamnet und per Packet-Radio gedacht.
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------

The DX-Cluster DB0LJ-6 moved to the Hamcloud.
This central place in the hamnet allows quick access from nearly all places in
the network.

Based on this move the hostnames and IP addresses have changed:
- from the hamnet:   dx.hamnet.cloud port 7300
- from the internet: dx.hamnet.network port 7300
- from Packet-Radio: "DB0LJ-6 via IGATE" or simply  "IGATE-6"

Scripts and filters have been copied from the former to the new system

This dx-cluster node in die Hamcloud system was mainly set up for users from
the hamnet and the packet-radio network.

! PI1LAP is Connected to the Reverse Beacon Network                   !

+---------------------------------------------------------------------+
! To allow RNB spots                                                  !
! set/skimmer # Allow all spots                                       !
! set/skimmer FT # Allow ft8 and ft4 spots                            !
! Skimmer options "BEACON, RTTY, PSK, DXF, CW, FT                     !
+---------------------------------------------------------------------+
! To only allow FT4 spots you can use a filter                        !
! set/skimmer ft                                                      !
! reject/rbn 1 info ft8                                               !
! show/filter # Show the rules in the filter                          !
! clear/rbn 1 or all # Delete filter rule                             !
+---------------------------------------------------------------------+
! To stop the normal Spots "unset/dx"                                 !
! To start the normal Spots "set/dx"                                  !
+---------------------------------------------------------------------+
! To post Dx Spots you have to be registerd on the Cluster.           !
! Send a mail with SP PD9Q or email PD9Q(@)Packet-radio.net           !

ACHTUNG: Das DX-CLuster DB0LJ-6 ist in die Hamcloud umgezogen.
Es ist dort zentral und schnell aus allen Teilen des Hamnets erreichbar.
Der Hostname hat sich geaendert: 
- im Hamnet:   dx.hamnet.cloud
- im Internet: dx.hamnet.network
- per Packet-Radio "DB0LJ-6 via IGATE" oder einfach "IGATE-6"

Scripte und Filter wurden aus der alten Datenbank transferiert.
Dieses DX-Cluster in der Hamcloud ist schwerpunktmaessig fuer die Nutzung aus
dem Hamnet und per Packet-Radio gedacht.
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------

The DX-Cluster DB0LJ-6 moved to the Hamcloud.
This central place in the hamnet allows quick access from nearly all places in
the network.

Based on this move the hostnames and IP addresses have changed:
- from the hamnet:   dx.hamnet.cloud port 7300
- from the internet: dx.hamnet.network port 7300
- from Packet-Radio: "DB0LJ-6 via IGATE" or simply  "IGATE-6"

Scripts and filters have been copied from the former to the new system

This dx-cluster node in die Hamcloud system was mainly set up for users from
the hamnet and the packet-radio network.

Running CC Cluster software version 3.397
Users can enter the following commands.
 
SET/SKIMMER Turns on skimmer spots.  This includes CW, RTTY, PSK, FT4 and
FT8. 
CW and RTTY are on by default.  The others need to be specifically turned on.
 
SET/NOSKIMMER Turns off skimmer spots.
 
SET/FT8  Turns on FT8 spots for both manually entered spots and skimmer
spots.
 
SET/NOFT8 turns them off.
 
You can also use similar commands for FT4, CW, RTTY, PSK.  These commands
look for the modes in the comments fields and have varying degrees of
success.  
Skimmer spots all include modes, so there is no problem with
them.  
Manual spots in the FT8 windows must include FT8 in the comments.
Other human entered spots should also include their mode.
 
Users must use SH/MYDX instead of SH/DX in order to repopulate their
screens with filtered spots.  
 
Entering SH/DX will give users everything and you will complain your filter is not working
Please enter your call:

! By default you will receive spots on following bands:
! HF + 6m + 4m ( 160m 80m 60m 40m 30m 20m 17m 15m 12m 10m 6m 4m )
!                                                                     
! By default you will receive spots from -or- to Europe. That means                        
! spotter or spotted callsign is within CQ zone 14,15,16,20
!
! You can customize the filters to suit your needs, see help files:
! --> http://wiki.dxcluster.org/index.php/Accept_filters 
! --> http://www.dxcluster.org/main/usermanual_en-10.html
!
! examples:
! ---------
! Show spots on HF bands all over the world, regardless zones:
! 'accept/spots on hf'
!
! Show spots on HF+6m heard in zones 3,4,5 (West/Center/East US):
! 'accept/spots on hf or 6m and (by_zone 3,4,5)'
!
! Show spots on HF bands heard in zones 3,4,5 (West/Center/East US)
! or the spotted callsigns are within zones 3,4,5:
! 'accept/spots on hf and (by_zone 3,4,5 or call_zone 3,4,5)'
!
! This node supports and maintains:

!

Hei,

CC-Cluster Commands for USERs
ANnounce 	Send an announcement to local USERs.  (AN <Text Message>)
ANnounce/Full 	Send an announcement to all nodes and USERs.  (AN/F <Text Message>)
BYE 	Disconnect from the node. (BYE) or (B)
DX 	Send a DX spot.  (DX <Callsign> <Frequency> or DX <Frequency> <Callsign>)
DXTEST 	Returns  to USER only. (DXT P5NOW 14006.06) Good for testing RES 1 & RES 2
DIR 	Shows mail messages on the node
DIR/BULLETIN 	Shows mail messages to ALL, BULLETIN and anything not to a call
DIR/NEW 	Shows only mail messages you haven't seen since your last DIR
DIR/OWN 	Shows only mail messages to you including messages to ALL & ones you sent
DIR/SUBJECT 	Shows mail messages with subject you enter.  (DIR/SUBJECT ARL)
DELete 	Delete mail messages. (DEL (Msg #) (DEL 1-99) Deletes your messages from 1 to 99
Kill 	Delete mail messages.  (K (Msg #) (K 1-99) Deletes your messages from 1 to 99
List 	Shows mail messages on the node
List/NEW 	Shows only mail messages you haven't seen since your last DIR or L
List/OWN 	Shows only mail messages to you including messages to ALL & ones you sent
QUIT 	Disconnect from the node
READ 	Read cluster mail.  (READ <Message #>)  See Mail Send/Receive below
REply 	REply without a number following replies to the last mail message you read.  REply <#> replies to the message with that number given.  REply/DELete replies to message and delets it.  REply/DELete/RR replies to message, delets message and gets a return receipt.  REply/RR replies to message and gets a return receipt. 
SEND 	(SEND <Callsign>) Sends mail  to that callsign. SEND <LOCAL> to just send a message to local node USERs.  SEND <ALL>, SEND <FORSALE> and SEND <DXNEWS> will be passed to all nodes for all USERs.
SET/ANN 	Turn on announcements
SET/BEACON 	Turn on beacon spots.  These are spots ending in /B or BCN
SET/BEEP 	Turn on a beep for DX and Announcement spots
SET/BOB 	Turn on bottom of band DX spots
SET/DX 	Turn on DX spot announcements
SET/DXCQ 	Turn on CQ Zone in DX info for DX spots
SET/DXITU 	Turn on ITU Zone in DX info for DX spots
SET/DXS 	Turn on US state/province or country in DX info for DX spots
SET/USSTATE 	Turn on US state or Canadian province spotter in DX info for DX spots
SET/FILTER 	See Band & Mode Filtering Below
SET/GRID 	Turns on DX Grid, toggles CQ Zone, ITU Zone, & US State to off
SET/HOME 	Tell cluster your home node. (SET/HOME <Node Call>) If you normally connect to K8SMC then it would be (SET/HOME K8SMC) 
SET/LOCATION 	Set your location (lat/lon) of your station.  (SET/LOCATION 42 17 N 84 21 W)
SET/LOGIN 	Tells cluster to send USER connects and disconnects.
SET/NAME 	Set your name (SET/NAME <First Name>)
SET/NOANN 	Turn off announcements.
SET/NOBEACON 	Turn off beacon spots.  These are spots ending in /B or BCN
SET/NOBEEP 	Turn off a beep for DX and Announcement spots .
SET/NOBOB 	Turn off bottom of band DX spots.
SET/NOCQ 	Turn off CQ Zone in spot announcements.
SET/NODX 	Turn off DX spot announcements.
SET/NODXCQ 	Turn off CQ Zone in DX info for DX spots
SET/NODXITU 	Turn off ITU Zone in DX info for DX spots
SET/NODXS 	Turn off US state/province or country in DX info for DX spots
SET/NOUSSTATE 	Turn off US state or Canadian province spotter in DX info for DX spots
SET/NOGRID 	Turn off DX Grids in spot announcements
SET/NOITU 	Turn off ITU Zone in spot announcements
SET/NOLOGIN 	Stops cluster from sending USER connects and disconnects
SET/NOSELF 	Turn off self spots by other users
SET/NOTALK 	Turn off the display of talk messages
SET/NOWCY 	Turn off the display of WCY spots
SET/NOWWV 	Turn off the display of WWV spots
SET/NOWX 	Turn off the display of weather announcements
SET/QRA 	Input your Grid Square. (SET/QRA EN72)
SET/QTH 	Set your city and state. (SET/QTH <City, State>) DX <City, Country>
SET/RES 1 	Tells CC-Cluster to give you 1 decimal point rounding in DX spots
SET/RES 2 	Tells CC-Cluster to give you 2 decimal point rounding in DX spots
SET/SELF 	Turn on self spots by other users
SET/TALK 	Turn on the display of talk messages
SET/USSTATE 	Turns on US State, toggles CQ Zone, DX Grid, & ITU Zone to off
SET/WCY 	Turn on the display of WCY spots
SET/WIDTH 	Sets the line width for DX spots, normally this has been 80 characters.  Depending on your logging program you can use anything between 45 to 130 characters,  SET/WIDTH XX where XX is the number of characters.
SET/WWV 	Turn on the display of WWV spots
SET/WX 	Turn on the display of weather announcements
SHow/CL 	Node Info and CCC Uptime  See SH/VERSION 
SHow/CONF 	Shows nodes and callsigns of USERs, only nodes called LOCAL by Sysop.
SHow/DX 	Shows last 30 spots
SHow/DX <Call> 	Shows last 30 spots for that call
SHow/DX/<number> 	Shows that number of spots.  SH/DX/100
SHow/DX/ <Band> 	Shows last 30 spots on that band.  SH/DX 20  for 20 meters
SHow/DX/ <Freq> 	Shows spots by frequency range.  Syntax = SH/DX 7020-7130
SHow/DX <prefix*> 	Shows all spots for a country, standard prefix not necessary, asterisk needed
SHow/DX 'rtty' 	Shows spots where the comment field contains (rtty)
SHow/FDX 	Shows last 30 real time formatted dx spots.
SHow/FILTER 	Shows how you have your filters set.
SHow/HEAD <Call> 	Shows heading - distance and bearing for the call.
SHow/MYANN 	Shows last 5 announcements allowed by your filter settings.
SHow/MYDX 	Shows last 30 spots allowed by your filter settings
SHow/MYDX <Call> 	Shows last 30 spots for the call allowed by your filter settings.
SHow/MYDX/<number> 	Shows that number of spots allowed by your filter.  SH/MYDX/100
SHow/MYDX/<Band> 	Shows last 30 spots on that band allowed by your filter settings.  SH/MYDX 20  for 20 meters
SHow/MYFDX 	Shows last 30 spots allowed by your filter settings.  
SHow/MYWX 	Shows last 5 weather announcements allowed by your filter settings.
SHow/RES 	Shows what your frequency decimal point rounding is set for.
SHow/SETTINGS 	Shows information on the node for your call and how you are setup.
SHow/STATION 	Shows information on the node for a station.  (SH/STA <Callsign>)
SHow/SUN 	Shows local sunrise and sunset times.  (SH/SUN <Prefix.) for that country
SHow/TIME 	Shows GMT time.
SHow/TIME <Call> 	Shows local time for the call.
SHow/USDB 	Shows State/Province for US/VE calls.  (SH/USDB <Callsign>)
SHow/USERS 	Shows callsigns of everyone connected to the local node.
SHow/VERSION 	Shows the CCC and Windsock Versions and Windsock Uptime for connections.
SHow/WIDTH 	Shows the amount of characters for DX spots in the comment field.
SHow/WWV 	Shows WWV info, (SH/WWV) gives last 5 (SH/WWV/99) gives last 99
SHow/WCY 	Shows last 5 DK0WCY, similar to WWV
Talk 	Send a talk message to someone on the node.  (T <Callsign> <Message>)
UNSET/ 	This command can be used instead of SET/NO, Compatibility for DX-Spider USERs
WHO 	This command will return a list of connections in alphabetical order.  Items are: Call User/Node Name IP/AGW
WX 	The command WX will send a local weather announcement.  (WX Sunny and Warm)

CC-Cluster Commands for USERs
ANnounce 	Send an announcement to local USERs.  (AN <Text Message>)
ANnounce/Full 	Send an announcement to all nodes and USERs.  (AN/F <Text Message>)
BYE 	Disconnect from the node. (BYE) or (B)
DX 	Send a DX spot.  (DX <Callsign> <Frequency> or DX <Frequency> <Callsign>)
DXTEST 	Returns  to USER only. (DXT P5NOW 14006.06) Good for testing RES 1 & RES 2
DIR 	Shows mail messages on the node
DIR/BULLETIN 	Shows mail messages to ALL, BULLETIN and anything not to a call
DIR/NEW 	Shows only mail messages you haven't seen since your last DIR
DIR/OWN 	Shows only mail messages to you including messages to ALL & ones you sent
DIR/SUBJECT 	Shows mail messages with subject you enter.  (DIR/SUBJECT ARL)
DELete 	Delete mail messages. (DEL (Msg #) (DEL 1-99) Deletes your messages from 1 to 99
Kill 	Delete mail messages.  (K (Msg #) (K 1-99) Deletes your messages from 1 to 99
List 	Shows mail messages on the node
List/NEW 	Shows only mail messages you haven't seen since your last DIR or L
List/OWN 	Shows only mail messages to you including messages to ALL & ones you sent
QUIT 	Disconnect from the node
READ 	Read cluster mail.  (READ <Message #>)  See Mail Send/Receive below
REply 	REply without a number following replies to the last mail message you read.  REply <#> replies to the message with that number given.  REply/DELete replies to message and delets it.  REply/DELete/RR replies to message, delets message and gets a return receipt.  REply/RR replies to message and gets a return receipt. 
SEND 	(SEND <Callsign>) Sends mail  to that callsign. SEND <LOCAL> to just send a message to local node USERs.  SEND <ALL>, SEND <FORSALE> and SEND <DXNEWS> will be passed to all nodes for all USERs.
SET/ANN 	Turn on announcements
SET/BEACON 	Turn on beacon spots.  These are spots ending in /B or BCN
SET/BEEP 	Turn on a beep for DX and Announcement spots
SET/BOB 	Turn on bottom of band DX spots
SET/DX 	Turn on DX spot announcements
SET/DXCQ 	Turn on CQ Zone in DX info for DX spots
SET/DXITU 	Turn on ITU Zone in DX info for DX spots
SET/DXS 	Turn on US state/province or country in DX info for DX spots
SET/USSTATE 	Turn on US state or Canadian province spotter in DX info for DX spots
SET/FILTER 	See Band & Mode Filtering Below
SET/GRID 	Turns on DX Grid, toggles CQ Zone, ITU Zone, & US State to off
SET/HOME 	Tell cluster your home node. (SET/HOME <Node Call>) If you normally connect to K8SMC then it would be (SET/HOME K8SMC) 
SET/LOCATION 	Set your location (lat/lon) of your station.  (SET/LOCATION 42 17 N 84 21 W)
SET/LOGIN 	Tells cluster to send USER connects and disconnects.
SET/NAME 	Set your name (SET/NAME <First Name>)
SET/NOANN 	Turn off announcements.
SET/NOBEACON 	Turn off beacon spots.  These are spots ending in /B or BCN
SET/NOBEEP 	Turn off a beep for DX and Announcement spots .
SET/NOBOB 	Turn off bottom of band DX spots.
SET/NOCQ 	Turn off CQ Zone in spot announcements.
SET/NODX 	Turn off DX spot announcements.
SET/NODXCQ 	Turn off CQ Zone in DX info for DX spots
SET/NODXITU 	Turn off ITU Zone in DX info for DX spots

If you just want to receive spots, you don't have to do anything.
 
Without registration you can still use the cluster and receive spots.
But, you will not be able to send spots into the network. To do that you
will have to drop me a line so I can enable you as a registered user.

call + password.

*****************************************************************************
************   WELCOME TO IZ2LSC xOTA  TELNET DX-CLUSTER   ******************
*                                                                           *
* This cluster node is connected to the Global DX spider network.           *
* In addition it forwards only to local users DX spot for                   *
*                    POTA, SOTA, BOTA, WWFF, IOTA                           *
*        programs feeded by respective clusters and RBN.                    *
*                                                                           *
* These spots are tagged with:                                              *
*           [-POTA-], [-SOTA-], [-WWBOTA-], [-WWFF-], [-IOTA-]              *
*                                                                           *
* Spots coming from the Reverse Beacon Network are tagged with [RBN]        *
*                                                                           *
* If you want to receive only POTA spots you can setup a filter             *
* with the command:                                                         *
*  ACC/SPOTS INFO -POTA-                                                    *
*                                                                           *
* If you want to receive only SOTA spots you can setup a filter             *
* with the command:                                                         *
*  ACC/SPOTS INFO -SOTA-                                                    *
*                                                                           *
* If you want to receive both SOTA and POTA spots you can setup a filter    *
* with the command:                                                         *
*  ACC/SPOTS INFO -SOTA- OR INFO -POTA-                                     *
*                                                                           *
* In a similar way you can use the other tags for other xOTA programs.      *
*                                                                           *
* To clear all the filters and receive all the spots use the command:       *
*  CLEAR/SPOTS ALL                                                          *
*                          _°_°_°_°_°_°_°_°_°_                              *
* This cluster implements advanced de-duplication and rate limiting         *
* algorithms to provide high quality spots by mitigating flooding and spam  *
* AI is used to detect and temporary blacklist users/nodes  sending         *
* harassment spots.                                                         *
*                         _°_°_°_°_°_°_°_°_°_                               *
*                                                                           *
* To have a better experience and read all the info available in the spot   *
* comment, set the terminal width to 120 with the command: set/width 120    *
*                                                                           *
*  >>> To send spot you must be register by sending a request email to <<<  *
*  >>>                       iz2lsc.andrea@gmail.com                   <<<  *
*                                                                           *
* --> This cluster will remain always free for everyone but if you like <-- *
* -->  this service and you want to contribute a bit to the opex costs  <-- *
* -->    please consider a paypal donation to andrea.timpy@gmail.com    <-- *
*                                                                           *
*    IT IS NOT ALLOWED TO USE [-POTA-], [-SOTA-], [-WWBOTA-], [-WWFF-],     *
*   [-IOTA-] TAGGED SPOTS GENERATED BY THIS CLUSTER TO FEED OTHER CLUSTER.  *
*                       USERS DOING THIS WILL BE BANNED.                    *
*                                                                           *
*                                                                           *
*****************************************************************************

DXSpider v1.57

========================================================
Welcome to DXContest.org Telnet Cluster Service
========================================================

All amateur radio spotting sources in one place:
- PSKReporter
- Reverse Beacon Network (RBN): CW & FT8/FT4 modes
- DX Cluster
- FreeDV Reporter

You can send spots to the DX Cluster network
using the DX command (e.g.: DX 14025.0 EA5WU CW)

[INFO] Beacon spots are filtered out and not displayed

FREE registration required at: https://dxcontest.org

Three ports available:
Port 7300: Traditional DX Cluster only (no filters)

Port 7310: For logging software (SwissLog, HRDLOG, etc)
Spots deduplicated every 10 minutes

Port 7320: For contest software (N1MM+, Win-Test, etc)
Specialized and optimized for contest operation

Configure your filters at (log in, "Telnet" tab):
https://dxcontest.org/monitor.html?page=telnet

Available commands after login:
DX freq call [info]            - Send a DX spot
SHOW/DX [n] or SH/DX [n]       - Show last n spots
SHOW/DX/20M or SH/DX/20M       - Show spots from specific band
STATS                          - Your connection statistics
FILTERS                        - Your active filters
SH/WWV                         - Solar data (SFI, A, K)
SH/U                           - Users connected now
SH/C                           - Node summary
SH/SUN / SH/MOON               - Sun and Moon from your QTH
SH/MUF                         - Observed activity by band
?                              - Show the command list
BYE or QUIT                    - Disconnect
========================================================

New commands:
set/skimmer   turns on Skimmer spots.
set/noskimmer turns off Skimmer spots.
set/ft8       turns on FT8 spots.
set/noft8     turns off FT8 spots.
set/own       turns on Skimmer spots for own call.
set/noown     turns them off.
set/nobeacon  turns off spots for beacons.
set/beacon    turns them back on.

Those commands make the cluster display the spotting station's 
locator if set by the spotting station. Substitute your own grid 
square when setting QRA. Thanks!  
 
* This DX node is currently employing a feed from the Reverse Beacon 
Network (RBN). Use  SET/SKIM to see RBN spots, SET/NOSKIM to turn 
them off. 
 
K7EK now operates a CW Skimmer that feeds W7JD-1 and RBN. 
 
NOTE: You must enable Skimmer spots every time you log in. I suggest 
automating this in your software by setting up a script to send 
SET/SKIM upon login. 
 
* To receive FT4 or FT8 spots:  SET/FT4  and  SET/FT8. To disable 
them type UNSET/FT4 and UNSET/FT8. 
 
* From time to time there will be disconnects. It is a fact of life. 
This may be due to internet related issues or Microsoft Windows 
automatic updates. Should that occur, simply reconnect. Please 
enable AUTOCONNECT or AUTORECONNECT in your client.  
 
* As an aid in keeping the connection operating, please enable 
KEEPALIVE. 
 
* This system has changed to a gigabit fiber connection
with static IP of 64.57.165.114. k7ek.ddns.org should resolve to
this static IP address. Use either for connecting to this system. 
Hopefully we will enjoy significantly improved service as a result.
 
* If you find yourself unable to reach k7ek.freeddns.org, k7ek.ddns.net,
or w7jd.ddns.net, please do a DNS FLUSH, restart your PC, and try 
again.

User commands: https://wiki.dxcluster.org/wiki/DXSpider_Filtering_Manual
----------------------------------------------------------------------------
The following commands and filter examples persist by login callsign and
SSID after they are set:
-
 Enable RBN spots enter: set/wantrbn
Disable RBN spots enter: unset/wantrbn
 Show US States in spot: set/usstate
----------------------------------------------------------------------------
Filter example to show spots outside USA(K) and Canada(VE), and only show spots
originating from USA(K) and Canada(VE):
-
For human spot filtering:
accept/spots by_dxcc k,ve
reject/spots dxcc k,ve
-
For RBN spot filtering:
accept/rbn by_dxcc k,ve
reject/rbn dxcc k,ve
----------------------------------------------------------------------------
Clear all filters: clear/spots all
----------------------------------------------------------------------------
Power Users, (reference the manual links above for your filter needs).

*** Connected to NC7J-5
****************************************************************************
			NG7M DX Cluster (AR-Cluster v6.6 Telnet Server)
             Located in Syracuse Utah at the QTH of NG7M
****************************************************************************
                 **** PLEASE READ THE FOLLOWING **** 
****************************************************************************
This DX Cluster is now using a special version of AR-Cluster v6 which can
provide both CW/RTTY skimmer spots and FT mode spots.

Your filters will need to change and use the FTSKIMMER command rather than
the previous SKIMMER command (both are now valid).  The SKIMMER command will
display CW/ RTTY skimmer spots and the ftskimmer command will display FT mode
spots. NOT SKIMMER will display human spots.
 
You can also use MODE to filter on CW,RTTY,FT8 and FT4.

For example: SET DX FILTER SKIMMER AND SPOTTERCTY=K OR FTSKIMMER AND
SPOTTERCTY=K AND MODE=FT8 would show CW/RTTY originating in the prefix K
and also show you FT8 mode spots originating in the prefix K.

THIS SKIMMER SERVER WILL EVENTUALLY BE SHUTDOWN because the main NC7J DX
Cluster will soon have the same functionality. Once you can nolonger connect
to this DX Cluster instance, START USING THE DX CLUSTER ON
HOST: DXC.NG7M.COM PORT 7300 AND USE THE NEW FTSKIMMER FILTER COMMAND SO 
YOU CAN GET CW/RTTY SKIMMER SPOTS, FT MODE SPOTS AND HUMAN SPOTS.

This notice was updated on 09/17/2026 by Max NG7M. This DX-Cluster node will
be disabled soon.
****************************************************************************
